              <span class="kicker">Step 2 of 4</span>
              <h1 style="font-size:26px;margin:8px 0 4px">How is this tax computed?</h1>
              <p class="hint">Choose the formula. Most sales-tax-style rates are a percent of the taxable subtotal. Flat and per-unit are common for regulatory or conservation fees.</p>

    if (prev.onboardingVariant !== this.props.onboardingVariant && this.props.onboardingVariant) this.setState({ onboardingVariant: this.props.onboardingVariant });
